== SRP 9sTh5h9c [OOP]   (turn, reach 1.00/1.00)
RANGE: You check almost your whole range here.
   - Why so little betting: against the 75% bet he sometimes folds (ace high (A♠4♠) and gutshots (Q♠8♠)), less than a bet of that size needs to pay with nothing behind it. He calls with top pair (Q♥T♠) and overpairs (K♥K♠), and about a quarter of your range is ahead of those hands. He raises now and then, with trips (A♦9♥) and top pair (A♦T♦).

   - Neither of you holds clearly more of the strong hands, though his range is ahead overall.

   - The bets are mostly open-ended straight draws (8♦7♦), trips (K♥9♥) and flush draws (K♥8♥). The checks are mostly top pair (K♠T♠) and ace high (A♠J♠).

   - When you check he bets about half the time, with ace high (A♦8♥) and gutshots (K♥J♦).
        [After your check he bets 46% of the time.]
   - What would change it. Take the strongest fifth of hands out of his range and you would bet about four fifths of yours instead of about a tenth: it is those hands of his that hold you back. If he could not bet when checked to you would bet about a quarter: the chance that he bets is part of what makes your checks worth making. If he could not raise you would bet about a quarter: his raises keep part of your range quiet. Without your own strongest fifth you would hardly bet at all: the rest of the betting leans on those hands being there.
